\displaystyle=\tau_{f}^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\big)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

while

\displaystyle\tau_{f}^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\opiGamma)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}},\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\,_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}}}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGamma\circ\muGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\pGammap\circ\muGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\overline{F}(\fX)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGamma\circ\muGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\circ\phi_{\fGX,\pGammap\circ\muGammap}^{\AGammap}.

In order to manipulate the (implicit) monster composition, we need to derive some identities with the compositor natural isomorphisms to simplify the expression. We will show that we can simplify the morphism

\displaystyle\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\overline{F}(\fX)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGamma\circ\muGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\pGammap\circ\muGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

by manipulating the coherences of the compositors.

For this we first calculate, using the pasting diagrams of pseudofunctors, that the diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGammap)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\Gamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\fX)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\fX\circ\pGamma\circ\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma)}

is equivalent to the pasting diagram:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGammap)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\fX)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\fX\circ\pGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\fX\circ\pGamma\circ\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)}

Note that in both cases, the
2
-cells are given by the corresponding compositors. This gives us that

\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma},\pGamma}^{\overline{F}(\fX)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGamma\circ\muGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}.

Similarly, the pasting diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGammap)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{(G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGammap)\circ\overline{F}(\pGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\opiGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGammap\circ\muGammap\circ\fGX)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\fGX)}

is equivalent to the pasting diagram:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGammap)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}^{\prime}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGammap\circ\muGammap\circ\fGX)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\fGX)\circ\overline{F}(\muGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGammap\circ\fGX)}

It follows that the
2
-cells are given by the corresponding compositors. This allows us to deduce that

\phi_{\fGX,\pGammap\circ\muGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\phi_{\muGammap,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}=\phi_{\muGammap\circ\fGX,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}.

This allows us to compute that

\displaystyle\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\overline{F}(\fX)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGamma\circ\muGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\pGammap\circ\muGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGammap\circ\fGX,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGammap\circ\fGX,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}.

We now use the same tricks to simplify the composition

\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGammap\circ\fGX,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}.

First note that
\muGammap\circ\fGX=\of_{c}\circ\muGamma
so

\phi_{\muGammap\circ\fGX,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}=\phi_{\of_{c}\circ\muGamma,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}.

Now because the pasting diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGammap)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}^{\prime}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGammap\circ\of_{c}\circ\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\of_{c}\circ\muGamma)}

is equivalent to

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGammap)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{(G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\of_{c})\circ\overline{F}(\pGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGammap\circ\of_{c})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGammap\circ\of_{c}\circ\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)}

we get that

\displaystyle\phi_{\muGammap\circ\fGX,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\of_{c}\circ\muGamma,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\pGammap\circ\of_{c}}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}^{\AGammap}.

Using this we derive that

\displaystyle\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGammap\circ\fGX,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\fX\circ\pGamma}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}^{\AGammap}.

In summary, we have shown so far that

\displaystyle\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\overline{F}(\fX)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}},\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\pi_{2}}}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\phi_{\muGammap,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}^{\AGammap}.

Altogether this allows us to deduce that

\displaystyle\tau^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\theta}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\overline{F}(\fX)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}},\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\pi_{2}}}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\phi_{\muGammap,\pGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\pGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\big)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\big)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}.

To proceed, we first observe that

\displaystyle\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{f}^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\theta}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)

\displaystyle=\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\big)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)

We will now study what happens when we post-compose this expression by
\overline{F}(\muGamma)\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}
. Explicitly,

\displaystyle\overline{F}(\muGamma)\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{\of_{c},\pGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\big)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\pGamma)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\cdots\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\fX\circ\pGamma}^{A}\circ\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\cdots\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\pGammap\circ\of_{c}}^{A}\circ\phi_{\pGamma,\of_{c}}^{\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\of_{c}}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\tau_{\pGammap}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\pGammap,\of_{c}}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\pGamma,\of_{c}}^{\AGammap}\overline{F}(\of_{c})\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\of_{c}}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\tau_{\pGammap}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{f_{c}}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\of_{c})\tau_{a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{A}\right)=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\aGammap\circ\of_{c}}^{A}\circ\phi_{f_{c},\aGammap}^{\AGammap}\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\fX\circ\aGamma}^{A}\circ\phi_{f_{c},\aGammap}^{\AGammap}\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\aGamma)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\aGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{f_{c},\aGammap}^{\AGammap}\right).

This implies in turn that

\displaystyle\overline{F}(\muGamma)\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{f}^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\theta}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\aGamma)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\aGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{f_{c},\aGammap}^{\AGammap}\right)

which allows us to deduce that

\displaystyle\tau_{f}^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\theta}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\aGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\aGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{f_{c},\aGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}.

To complete the proof that
\Theta
is an
F_{G}(X)
-morphism, it suffices at this point to show that the equality

\displaystyle\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\aGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\aGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{f_{c},\aGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\oalphaGamma)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\oalphaGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\oalphaGammap}^{\AGammap}

holds. However, this follows from an extremely tedious manipulation of the naturality of the compositors and the relations on the compositors as induced by the pseudofunctoriality of
\overline{F}
and Lemma
6.2.12
. From this it follows that

\displaystyle\tau_{f}^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\theta}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\big(\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\aGamma)\big)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\phi_{\aGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\phi_{f_{c},\aGammap}^{\AGammap}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\of_{c}}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\fGX,\muGammap}^{\overline{F}(\aGammap)\AGammap}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\left(\phi_{\muGammap,a_{\Gamma^{\prime}}}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\oalphaGamma)\tau_{f}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\oalphaGamma,\fX}^{\AGammap}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\circ\phi_{\fGX,\oalphaGammap}^{\AGammap}

\displaystyle=\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\tau_{f}^{\alpha_{X}^{\ast}A}.

This proves, finally, that
\tau_{f}^{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A}\circ\overline{F}(\fGX)\,_{\Gamma^{\prime}}{\theta}=\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\tau_{f}^{\alpha_{X}^{\ast}A}
and hence shows that
\Theta\in F_{G}(X)_{1}
. That
\Theta
is an isomorphism is immediate from each
\,{}_{\Gamma}{\theta}
being an isomorphism, which proves that there is an isomorphism

\alpha_{X}^{\ast}A\xrightarrow[\Theta]{\cong}\pi_{2}^{\ast}A

in
F_{G}(G\times X)
for any
A\in F_{G}(X)_{0}
.

Having finally proved that
\Theta
is an
F_{G}(X)
-isomorphism, we now show that the
\Theta
are natural in
A
. For this fix a
P:A\to B
in
F_{G}(X)
. Fix a
\Gamma\in\Sf(G)_{0}
and note that we must check that the diagram

\textstyle{\alpha_{X}^{\ast}A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\alpha_{X}^{\ast}P}
\scriptstyle{\Theta_{A}}
\textstyle{\pi_{2}^{\ast}A\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\pi_{2}^{\ast}P}
\textstyle{\alpha_{X}^{\ast}B\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\Theta_{B}}
\textstyle{\pi_{2}^{\ast}B}

commutes. We calculate

\displaystyle\overline{F}(\opiGamma)\rhoGamma\circ\,_{\Gamma}{\theta_{A}}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\opiGamma)\rhoGamma\circ\phi_{\muGamma,p_{\Gamma}}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,a_{\Gamma}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,p_{\Gamma}}^{\BGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\overline{F}(\pGamma)\rhoGamma\circ\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,a_{\Gamma}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,p_{\Gamma}}^{\BGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}}^{B}\right)^{-1}\circ\rhoGamma\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,a_{\Gamma}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,p_{\Gamma}}^{\BGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}}^{B}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}}^{B}\right)\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\overline{F}(\aGamma)\rhoGamma\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,a_{\Gamma}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,p_{\Gamma}}^{\BGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}}^{B}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}}^{B}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,a_{\Gamma}}^{\BGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma)\rhoGamma

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma,p_{\Gamma}}^{\BGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}}^{B}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}}^{B}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,a_{\Gamma}}^{\BGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\oalphaGamma)\rhoGamma

\displaystyle=\,_{\Gamma}{\theta_{B}}\circ\overline{F}(\oalphaGamma)\rhoGamma,

which verifies the commutativity of the diagram.
∎

Corollary 6.2.15
.

For any left
G
-variety
X
or any left
G
-space
X
and any pre-equivariant pseudofunctor
F
on
X
, there is a natural isomorphism of functors
\pi_{2}^{\ast}\cong\alpha_{X}^{\ast}:F_{G}(X)\to F_{G}(G\times X)
.

Before proving the cocycle condition that
\Theta
satisfies, we actually define it so that we know what we must
actually
prove.

Definition 6.2.16
.

Let
F
be a pre-equivariant pseudofunctor on
X
, let
A\in F_{G}(X)_{0}
, and let
\Theta:\alpha_{X}^{\ast}A\to\pi_{2}^{\ast}A
be an isomorphism in
F_{G}(G\times X)
. We say that
\Theta
satisfies the GIT-cocycle condition if, for morphisms

\displaystyle d_{0}^{1}

\displaystyle:=\pi_{23}:G\times G\times X\to G\times X;

\displaystyle d_{1}^{1}

\displaystyle:=\mu_{G}\times\id_{X}:G\times G\times X\to G\times X;

\displaystyle d_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle:=\id_{G}\times\alpha_{X}:G\times G\times X\to G\times X

for all
\Gamma\in\Sf(G)_{0}
the composite

\displaystyle\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ F(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)

is equal to the composite

\displaystyle\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ F(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ F(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}.

Let us now motivate the GIT cocycle condition by studying how it arose. In
[
47
]
the authors described an equivariance isomorphism on a sheaf
\Fscr
on
X
as an isomorphism

\theta:\alpha_{X}^{\ast}\Fscr\xrightarrow{\cong}\pi_{2}^{\ast}\Fscr

in
\Shv(G\times X)
. This isomorphism
\theta
was used to illustrate the how the action of
G
on
X
affects the space
X
and the sheaf
\Fscr
, as well as how these conditions need to be suitably compatible in order to define an equivariant sheaf. Let us describe this more explicitly. The action
\alpha_{X}
needs to intertwine the multiplication of the group
G
on itself together with the action on
X
in the sense that the diagram

\textstyle{G\times G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\id_{G}\times\alpha_{X}}
\scriptstyle{\mu_{G}\times\id_{X}}
\textstyle{G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\alpha_{X}}
\textstyle{G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\alpha_{X}}
\textstyle{X}

commutes. When looking at this at the level of stalks of
\Fscr
, this requires an isomorphism

\Fscr_{x}\cong\Fscr_{(gh)x}\cong\Fscr_{g(hx)}

for any stalk
(g,h,x)\in G\times G\times X
. However, these isomorphisms should come from the equivariance
\theta
lifted to
G\times G\times X
by either changing the order of multiplication, action, and projection more-or-less freely (provided we do so in a way that makes sense). There are three maps from
G\times G\times X
to
G\times X
that we use to lift
\theta
; these three maps coincide with the simplicial presentation of both the quotient variety
\underline{G\backslash X}_{\bullet}
and the simplicial information encoded by the internal translation groupoid
\mathbb{G\times X}
(cf.
[
63
, Appendix B]
. These three maps, together with the two maps from
G\times X\to X
in the diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\times G\times X}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\times X}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces X}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\pi_{23}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{G}\times\id_{X}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\id_{G}\times\alpha_{X}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\pi_{2}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\alpha_{X}}

satisfy the equations

\displaystyle\alpha_{X}\circ(\mu_{G}\times\id_{X})

\displaystyle=\alpha_{X}\circ(\id_{G}\times\alpha_{X});

\displaystyle\alpha_{X}\circ\pi_{23}

\displaystyle=\pi_{2}\circ(\id_{G}\times\alpha_{X});

\displaystyle\pi_{2}\circ\pi_{23}

\displaystyle=\pi_{2}\circ(\mu_{G}\times\id_{X}).

Define, in order to save space and illustrate the simplicial connection, the morphisms

\displaystyle\pi_{23}

\displaystyle:=d_{0}^{1};

\displaystyle\mu_{G}\times\id_{X}

\displaystyle:=d_{1}^{1};

\displaystyle\id_{G}\times\alpha_{X}

\displaystyle:=d_{2}^{1}.

Using these maps and relations we then can produce a diagram (assuming that the underlying pseudofunctor
\Shv(-)
is strict in this case)

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{2}^{1})^{\ast}\big(\alpha_{X}^{\ast}\Fscr\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{2}^{1})^{\ast}\big(\pi_{2}^{\ast}\Fscr\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\pi_{2}\circ d_{2}^{1}\right)^{\ast}\Fscr}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\alpha_{X}\circ d_{2}^{1})^{\ast}\Fscr}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\alpha_{X}\circ d_{0}^{1})^{\ast}\Fscr}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\alpha_{X}\circ d_{1}^{1})^{\ast}\Fscr}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{0}^{1})^{\ast}\big(\alpha_{X}^{\ast}\Fscr\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{1}^{1})^{\ast}\alpha_{X}^{\ast}\Fscr}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{0}^{1})^{\ast}\big(\pi_{2}^{\ast}\Fscr\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\pi_{2}\circ d_{0}^{1})^{\ast}\Fscr}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{1}^{1})^{\ast}\big(\pi_{2}^{\ast}\Fscr\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\pi_{2}\circ d_{1}^{1})^{\ast}\Fscr}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{2}^{1})^{\ast}\theta}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{0}^{1})^{\ast}\theta}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(d_{1}^{1})^{\ast}\theta}

The cocycle condition of
[
47
]
asks exactly that the above diagram commute, i.e.,

(d_{1}^{1})^{\ast}\theta=(d_{0}^{1})^{\ast}\theta\circ(d_{2}^{1})^{\ast}\theta.

Remark 6.2.17
.

In the case when
\Fscr
is a sheaf on a topological space
X
, the GIT cocycle condition is equivalent to asking that if
\operatorname{esp}(\Fscr)
is the espace étalé of
\Fscr
, then
\operatorname{esp}(\Fscr)
carries a
G
-action for which the étale map of spaces
p:\operatorname{esp}(\Fscr)\to X
is
G
-equivariant.

Our pseudofunctorial GIT-cocycle condition then asks for a pre-equivariant pseudofunctor
F
over
X
that for any
A\in F_{G}(X)_{0}
and
\Gamma\in\Sf(G)_{0}
, the diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\oalphaGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})(\AGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\big(F(\oalphaGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\oalphaGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})(\AGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\big(F(\opiGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\big(F(\oalphaGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\opiGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\big(F(\opiGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\oalphaGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\AGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\opiGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})(\AGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\big(F(\oalphaGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\opiGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\AGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\big(F(\opiGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}}

commutes. Note that while we have not proved it explicitly, it follows mutatis mutandis to Lemmas
6.2.6
and
6.2.7
that
F
admits pseudocone translations all of
d_{0}^{1},

d_{1}^{1},
and
d_{2}^{1}
. This gives rise to induce the corresponding pullback functors

(d_{0}^{1})^{\ast},(d_{1}^{1})^{\ast},(d_{2}^{1})^{\ast}:F_{G}(G\times X)\to F_{G}(G\times G\times X).

As we move to show that the isomorphism
\Theta
of Theorem
6.2.14
satisfies the GIT cocycle condtion (cf. Theorem
6.2.20
below), we will need a short lemma on the interaction of the maps
\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}
and
\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}
with
\muGamma
.

Lemma 6.2.18
.

For any
\Gamma\in\Sf(G)_{0}
when
G
is a smooth algebraic group or
M\in\mathbf{FAMan}(G)_{0}
when
G
is a topological group, if
\muGamma
is the isomorphism of Lemma
6.2.12
and
\mu_{M}
is the isomorphism of Lemma
6.2.13
, then

\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}=\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}

and

\mu_{M}\circ\,_{M}{\overline{d}_{0}^{1}}=\mu_{M}\circ\,_{M}{\overline{d}_{2}^{1}}.

Proof.

Observe that since

\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}}=\pi_{134}:\Gamma\times G\times G\times X\to\Gamma\times G\times X

and

\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}}=\id_{\Gamma\times G}\times\alpha_{X}:\Gamma\times G\times G\times X\to\Gamma\times G\times X

there are unique morphisms making the diagrams

\textstyle{\Gamma\times G\times G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}}}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}}}
\scriptstyle{\quo_{\Gamma\times G\times G}}
\textstyle{\Gamma\times G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\quo_{G\times\Gamma}}
\textstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}
\textstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)}}

\textstyle{\Gamma\times G\times G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}}}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}}}
\scriptstyle{\quo_{(\Gamma\times G)_{c}}}
\textstyle{\Gamma\times G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\quo_{\Gamma_{c}}}
\textstyle{\,{}_{(\Gamma\times G)_{c}}{X}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})_{c}}
\scriptstyle{(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})_{c}}
\textstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{X}}

commute. However, since the second diagram factors through the diagrams

\textstyle{\Gamma\times G\times G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}}}

\scriptstyle{\quo_{(\Gamma\times G)_{c}}}
\textstyle{\Gamma\times G\times X\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\quo_{\Gamma_{c}}}
\textstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}
\scriptstyle{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}
\scriptstyle{\mu_{\Gamma\times G}}
\textstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\muGamma}
\textstyle{\,{}_{(\Gamma\times G)_{c}}{X}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})_{c}}
\scriptstyle{(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})_{c}}
\textstyle{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{X}}

the result follows.
∎

Corollary 6.2.19
.

For any pre-equivariant pseudofunctor
F
over
X
,

\overline{F}\left(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}\right)=\overline{F}\left(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}\right)

and

\overline{F}\left(\mu_{M}\circ\,_{M}{\overline{d}_{0}^{1}}\right)=\overline{F}\left(\mu_{M}\circ\,_{M}{\overline{d}_{2}^{1}}\right)

Theorem 6.2.20
.

Let
F
be a pre-equivariant pseudofunctor over
X
and let
A\in F_{G}(X)_{0}
. Then the equivariance
\Theta
of Theorem
6.2.14
satisfies the GIT cocycle condition.

Proof.

As usual, we prove the variety case and omit the topological case, as the topological case follows mutatis mutandis. Begin by noting that from Theorem
6.2.14
we must prove that

\displaystyle\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)

\displaystyle=\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\big)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{1}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)

is equal to the composite

\displaystyle\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}.

We will prove this by systematically attacking and simplifying the second expression. Begin by recalling that either by Lemma
6.2.12
or the exposition prior to the Lemma (respectively Lemma
6.2.13
), the identities

\displaystyle\opiGamma

\displaystyle=\pGamma\circ\muGamma;

\displaystyle\oalphaGamma

\displaystyle=\aGamma\circ\muGamma.

both hold. Now, using these and the identities from before the statment of the theorem, we observe as in the proof of Thoerem
6.2.14
that the pasting diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\opiGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}

is equal to the pasting diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}

where the the
2
-cells are again the compositor isomorphisms. This allows us to deduce that

\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}.

Similarly, using that

\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}=\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\right)^{-1}

and proceeding to analyze the pasting diagrams as before, we get that

\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}=\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}.

We also derive that

\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}

and

\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}=\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

through the same technique. Putting these together we get that

\displaystyle\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}.

To simplify the expression above, we now focus on manipulating the transition isomorphisms and their interactions with the compositor natural isomorphisms. To begin this process note that since the diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\big(\overline{F}(\aGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{A})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\big(\overline{F}(\aGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{A})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\right)^{-1}}

the equation

\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}=\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}

holds. Similarly, the naturality of the compositors and their inverses allows us to derive that

\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\right)^{-1}=\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}

which allows us to further calculate that

\displaystyle\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\big((\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\big)

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\big((\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\big).

Now by using Corollary
6.2.19
we get that

\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{A})=\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}).

Using this we derive that the diagram

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\AGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})(\AGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\big(\overline{F}(\aGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\big(\overline{F}(\pGamma)(\AGamma)\big)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{A})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{A})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}}

commutes which tells us that

\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}=\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}.

This allows us to compute that

\displaystyle\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}.

Using these various calculations we find that

\displaystyle\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}

\displaystyle\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\big((\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\big)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}

\displaystyle\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left(\big(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A}\big)^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)\AGamma}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}}}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}.

To further manipulate the expression above into our desired format, we will need some calculations to translate between the various compositions of
\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})
and
\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})
(and similarly with
\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})
and
\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})
). Note that because

\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})=\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})

we have the
2
-commutative diagram:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}

These allow us to deduce that the (equivalent) pasting diagrams

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\opiGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})}

are equal to the (also equivalent) pasting diagrams

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)\circ\overline{F}(\pGammap)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\opiGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\pGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}

and hence deduce that

\displaystyle\phi_{\opiGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}.

Similarly, from the equality

\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})=\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})

we get the
2
-cell

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\XGamma)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times G\times X)})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{X})}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\aGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\aGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\aGamma\circ\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\muGamma)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})}

which allows us to deduce that

\displaystyle\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\oalphaGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}.

Before we can complete the proof of the theorem, we need three last observations. First note that by using the compositor isomorphisms we have that

\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}=\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

and similarly that

\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}=\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\right)^{-1}.

However, using that

\displaystyle\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}

\displaystyle=\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{A}}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}

\displaystyle=\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}

\displaystyle=\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left((\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)

we derive that

\displaystyle\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{0}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\opiGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{d_{2}^{1}},\oalphaGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}})(\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}})\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{0}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\pGamma)\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left((\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\muGamma}^{\overline{F}(\aGamma)\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\muGamma\circ\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{2}^{1}},\aGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\opiGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\circ\big(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\circ\overline{F}(\muGamma)\big)\left((\tau_{\pGamma}^{A})^{-1}\circ\tau_{\aGamma}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\phi_{\muGamma,\pGamma}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}\circ\left(\phi_{\oalphaGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\opiGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\circ\overline{F}(\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}})\,_{\Gamma}{\theta}\circ\left(\phi_{\oalphaGamma,\,_{{\Gamma}}{\overline{\mathnormal{d}}_{1}^{1}}}^{\AGamma}\right)^{-1}.

This establishes the GIT cocycle condition.
∎

Definition 6.2.21
.

Let
G
be a smooth algebraic group and let
X
be a left
G
-variety or let
G
be a topological group and let
X
be a left
G
-space. If
(F,\overline{F})
is a pre-equivariant pseudofunctor on
X
and
A\in F_{G}(X)_{0}
then we say that the natural isomorphism
\Theta^{A}
is the equivariance on
A
.

We now close this chapter with a short comparison between how the equivariance
\Theta
attached to a pre-equivariant pseudofunctor
(F,\overline{F})
interacts with a
G
-equivariant morphism
h:X\to Y
. Because a pre-equivariant pseudofunctor
(F,\overline{F})
attached to a variety
X
comes equipped with the pseudofunctor
\overline{F}:\Var_{/K}^{\op}\to\fCat
, there is another pre-equivariant pseudofunctor
(F^{\prime},\overline{F})
on
Y
with
F^{\prime}=\overline{F}\circ\quo_{G\backslash(-)}:\SfResl_{G}(Y)^{\op}\to\fCat
(and similarly for the topological situation). The fact that the morphism
h:X\to Y
is equivariant implies that the constructions in Lemma
6.2.12
are natural in the sense that for any
\Gamma
in
\Sf(G)_{0}
the diagrams

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{X}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{X}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times Y)}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{Y}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{Y}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\backslash(\id_{\Gamma\times G}\times f)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\backslash(\id_{\Gamma_{c}}\times f)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces a_{\Gamma}^{X}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\backslash(\id_{\Gamma}\times f)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}

and

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times X)}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{X}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{X}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{(G\times Y)}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{Y}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\,{}_{\Gamma}{Y}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\backslash(\id_{\Gamma\times G}\times f)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\backslash(\id_{\Gamma_{c}}\times f)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces p_{\Gamma}^{X}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces G\backslash(\id_{\Gamma}\times f)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}

both commute. Because of this we can apply the formalism of Theorem
4.2.6
to give rise to functors
h^{\ast}:F_{G}(Y)\to F_{G}(X)
and
(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}:F_{G}(G\times Y)\to F_{G}(G\times X)
. We can then consider the two distinct equivariances

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F_{G}(X)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F_{G}(G\times X)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\alpha_{X}^{\ast}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\pi_{2}^{X})^{\ast}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\Theta_{X}}

and:

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F_{G}(Y)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces F_{G}(G\times Y)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\alpha_{X}^{\ast}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces(\pi_{2}^{Y})^{\ast}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\Theta_{Y}}

By pre-and-post whiskering by pullbacks along
h
in different ways we get natural isomorphisms

\Theta_{X}\ast h^{\ast}:\alpha_{X}^{\ast}\circ h^{\ast}\xRightarrow{\cong}\left(\pi_{2}^{X}\right)^{\ast}\circ h^{\ast}

and

(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}\ast\Theta_{Y}:(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}\circ\alpha_{Y}^{\ast}\xRightarrow{\cong}(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}\circ\left(\pi_{2}^{Y}\right)^{\ast}.

However, because every pullback functor in sight is induced by
F
and
\Gamma
-locally has the form

\,{}_{\Gamma}{\left(\alpha_{X}^{\ast}\circ h^{\ast}\right)\left(A\right)}=\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{\alpha_{X}}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{h}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right),

\,{}_{\Gamma}{\left(\left(\id_{G}\times h\right)^{\ast}\circ\alpha_{Y}^{\ast}\right)\left(A\right)}=\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{\overline{h}}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{\alpha_{Y}}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)

and similarly for the
\pi_{2}
versions. However, because
h
is
G
-equivariant, we have that
\alpha_{Y}\circ(\id_{G}\times h)=h\circ\alpha_{X}
and so there are natural isomorphisms

\alpha_{X}^{\ast}\circ h^{\ast}\cong\left(\id_{G}\times h\right)^{\ast}\circ\alpha_{Y}^{\ast}

and similarly

\left(\pi_{2}^{X}\right)^{\ast}\circ h^{\ast}\cong(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}\circ\left(\pi_{2}^{Y}\right)^{\ast}.

This leads to a natural question: how different are the distinct pullbacks of the equivariances? More explicitly, what is the difference, if any, between the natural isomorphisms

(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}\ast\Theta_{Y}

and

\Theta_{X}\ast h^{\ast}

when they are manipulated through the compositors to the pullback functor
(h\circ\alpha_{X})^{\ast}=(\alpha_{Y}\circ(\id_{G}\times h))^{\ast}
? We give an explicit answer in Proposition
6.2.22
below and show there is no difference in the strict pseudofunctor case in Corollary
6.2.23
.

Proposition 6.2.22
.

For any smooth algebraic group
G
and
G
-equivariant morphism of varieties
h:X\to Y
, if
(\overline{F}\circ\quo_{X}^{\op},\overline{F})
and
(\overline{F}\circ\quo_{Y}^{\op},\overline{F})
are pre-equivariant morphisms on
X
and
Y
, respectively, then the natural isomorphisms
(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}(\Theta_{Y}^{A})
and
\Theta_{X}^{h^{\ast}A}
satisfy the equation

\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h},\,_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}^{Y}}}^{A}\circ\,_{\Gamma}{(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}\left(\Theta_{Y}^{A}\right)}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h},\,_{\Gamma}{\alpha_{Y}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}=\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}^{X}},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\circ\,_{\Gamma}{\Theta_{X}^{h^{\ast}A}}\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{\alpha_{X}},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)^{-1}

for all
\Gamma\in\Sf(G)_{0}
and for every
A\in F_{G}(Y)_{0}
. The same holds for topological groups
G
, left
G
-spaces
X
, and all
M\in\mathbf{FAMan}(G)_{0}
.

Proof.

We prove this for the geometric case, as the topological case follows mutatis mutandis. The pseudofunctoriality of the pre-equivariant pseudofunctor
(F,\overline{F})
implies that for any object
A
of
F_{G}(Y)
and for any
\Gamma\in\Sf(G)_{0}
the diagrams

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{h})\left(\overline{F}(p_{\Gamma}^{Y})\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)\right)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\circ\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\overline{F}\left(p_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\overline{F}\left(p_{\Gamma}^{Y}\circ\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}\circ\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{\overline{h}}\right)\left(\overline{F}\left(p_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(p_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{h}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{\overline{h}}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(p_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}^{X}}\right)\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{h}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{\overline{\pi}_{2}^{Y}}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{h}\circ\,_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}^{X}}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}}^{\overline{F}(p_{\Gamma}^{Y})A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\phi_{p_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y},\,_{\Gamma\times G}{h}}^{\overline{F}(p_{\Gamma}^{Y})A}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},p_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y},p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}^{X}},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h},\,_{\Gamma}{\pi_{2}^{Y}}}^{A}\right)^{-1}}

and

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(a_{\Gamma}^{Y}\circ\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(a_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{\alpha_{Y}}\circ\,_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(a_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(a_{\Gamma}^{X}\circ\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{h}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(a_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(a_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{h}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{h}\circ a_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{A}\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h},\,_{\Gamma}{\alpha_{Y}}}^{A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h},\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{\overline{F}(a_{\Gamma}^{Y})A}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma}{\alpha_{X}},\,_{\Gamma\times G}{h}}^{A}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}}^{\overline{F}(a_{\Gamma}^{Y})A}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},a_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\right)^{-1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\phi_{a_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}\right)}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}}

both commute. We then calculate on one hand that

\,{}_{\Gamma}{\Theta^{h^{\ast}A}_{X}}=\,_{\Gamma}{\theta^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}_{X}}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},p_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{h^{\ast}A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{h^{\ast}A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},a_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},p_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)\circ\phi_{p_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\left(\tau_{a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{a_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},a_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},p_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\left(\phi_{p_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\right)\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\circ\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\right)\left(\tau_{a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{a_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},a_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\right)^{-1}

which we suggestively display as

\,{}_{\Gamma}{\Theta^{h^{\ast}A}_{X}}

\displaystyle=\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},p_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\left(\phi_{p_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\left(\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\circ\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h}\right)\right)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)

\displaystyle\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{X}\right)\left(\left(\phi_{\,{}_{\Gamma_{c}}{h},a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\phi_{a_{\Gamma}^{X},\,_{\Gamma}{h}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{X},a_{\Gamma}^{X}}^{\overline{F}(\,_{\Gamma}{h})A}\right)^{-1}

On the other hand we calculate

\,{}_{\Gamma}{(\id_{G}\times h)^{\ast}\left(\theta_{Y}^{A}\right)}=\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)\left(\,{}_{\Gamma}{\theta_{Y}^{A}}\right)

\displaystyle=\overline{F}\left(\,{}_{\Gamma\times G}{h}\right)\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y},p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\circ\overline{F}\left(\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y}\right)\left(\left(\tau_{p_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\circ\tau_{a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)\circ\left(\phi_{\mu_{\Gamma}^{Y},a_{\Gamma}^{Y}}^{A}\right)^{-1}\right)
